- **raci-responsibility-definition**: Responsibility / decision-rights matrices. RACI (Responsible / Accountable / Consulted / Informed — exactly one A per row) for ongoing activities. RAPID (Recommend / Agree / Perform / Input / Decide — one D + sparing A) for consequential decisions. DACI for lighter single-decision. Anti-patterns (multiple As, all-Cs paralysis, same-person R+A bottleneck, generic rows, wrong-framework-for-question). Quarterly review + owner + version. Not a staffing plan. ([SSiertsema/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/SSiertsema/claude-code-plugins/tree/HEAD/raci-responsibility-definition))
